{"timestamp":"2026-07-28T22:26:30.625Z","type":"response_item","payload":{"type":"function_call","id":"exec_command:138","name":"exec_command","arguments":"{\"cmd\": \"cd /Users/olivierhabimana/Desktop/projects/tellus-oss-v2-verify-6454c2a && cat > scripts/oss-v2-subscription-soak.mjs <<'EOF'\\n// One-hour durable ObjectSet subscription soak.\\n// Opens N concurrent subscriptions across the two API nodes, generates\\n// source events via real v2 action executions, and verifies durable\\n// delivery, replay-after-reconnect, cursor monotonicity, tenant isolation,\\n// and bounded resource usage. Acceptance criteria are declared up front.\\nimport { writeFile, appendFile } from \\\"node:fs/promises\\\";\\nimport WebSocket from \\\"ws\\\";\\n\\nconst token = process.env.TELLUS_ACCEPTANCE_TOKEN;\\nif (!token) throw new Error(\\\"TELLUS_ACCEPTANCE_TOKEN required\\\");\\nconst ports = (process.env.TELLUS_API_PORTS ?? \\\"3400,3401\\\").split(\\\",\\\").map(Number);\\nconst ontology = process.env.TELLUS_ONTOLOGY ?? \\\"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\\\";\\nconst tenant = process.env.TELLUS_TENANT ?? \\\"default\\\";\\nconst subscriptionCount = Number(process.env.SUBSCRIPTION_COUNT ?? 100);\\nconst durationMs = Number(process.env.DURATION_MS ?? 3_600_000);\\nconst eventIntervalMs = Number(process.env.EVENT_INTERVAL_MS ?? 5_000);\\nconst reconnectProbeEveryMs = Number(process.env.RECONNECT_PROBE_EVERY_MS ?? 300_000);\\nconst outputPath = process.env.OUTPUT_PATH;\\nconst ndjsonPath = process.env.NDJSON_PATH;\\n\\nconst slo = {\\n  lostEvents: 0,\\n  unauthorizedEvents: 0,\\n  unexplainedCursorRegressions: 0,\\n  maxSubscriptions: subscriptionCount,\\n  eventDeliveryP99Ms: 10_000,\\n  note: \\\"at-least-once duplicates acceptable only with stable eventIds and client dedup\\\",\\n};\\n\\nconst metrics = {\\n  subscriptionsOpened: 0,\\n  subscriptionsFailedToOpen: 0,\\n  subscriptionsClosedByServer: 0,\\n  reconnects: 0,\\n  resumes: 0,\\n  sourceActions: 0,\\n  sourceActionFailures: 0,\\n  deliveredFrames: 0,\\n  deliveredEvents: 0,\\n  duplicateEvents: 0,\\n  lostEvents: 0,\\n  unauthorizedEvents: 0,\\n  cursorRegressions: 0,\\n  refreshOrResyncFrames: 0,\\n  errorFrames: [],\\n  deliveryLatencyMs: [],\\n  perSubscription: {},\\n  resourceSamples: [],\\n  startedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\\n};\\n\\nconst employee = { type: \\\"base\\\", objectType: \\\"Employee\\\" };\\nconst seenEventIds = new Set();\\nconst pendingSourceEvents = new Map(); // employeeId -> action completion time\\nconst deadline = Date.now() + durationMs;\\nlet stopped = false;\\n\\nfunction log(event) {\\n  const line = JSON.stringify({ at: new Date().toISOString(), ...event });\\n  if (ndjsonPath) void appendFile(ndjsonPath, line + \\\"\\\\n\\\").catch(() => {});\\n}\\n\\nasync function apiPost(port, route, body) {\\n  const started = performance.now();\\n  const res = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/v2/ontologies/${ontology}/${route}`, {\\n    method: \\\"POST\\\",\\n    headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, \\\"content-type\\\": \\\"application/json\\\", \\\"x-tellus-tenant\\\": tenant },\\n    body: JSON.stringify(body),\\n  });\\n  const text = await res.text();\\n  let parsed = null;\\n  try { parsed = JSON.parse(text); } catch {}\\n  return { status: res.status, body: parsed, ms: performance.now() - started };\\n}\\n\\nclass SoakSubscription {\\n  constructor(index) {\\n    this.index = index;\\n    this.port = ports[index % ports.length];\\n    this.id = null;\\n    this.cursor = null;\\n    this.sequence = -1;\\n    this.frames = 0;\\n    this.events = 0;\\n    this.state = \\\"init\\\";\\n    this.ws = null;\\n  }\\n  async connect(resume = false) {\\n    const url = `ws://127.0.0.1:${this.port}/api/v2/ontologies/${ontology}/objectSets/stream`;\\n    this.ws = new WebSocket(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, \\\"x-tellus-tenant\\\": tenant } });\\n    await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\\n      const t = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(\\\"ack timeout\\\")), 15_000);\\n      this.ws.once(\\\"open\\\", () => {\\n        if (resume && this.id && this.cursor != null) {\\n          metrics.resumes += 1;\\n          this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: \\\"resume\\\", id: `soak-resume-${this.index}-${Date.now()}`, subscriptionId: this.id, cursor: this.cursor }));\\n          clearTimeout(t); resolve();\\n        } else {\\n          this.ws.send(JSON.stringify({\\n            type: \\\"subscribeRequests\\\",\\n            id: `soak-${this.index}-${Date.now()}`,\\n            requests: [{ objectSet: employee, propertySet: [\\\"employeeId\\\", \\\"name\\\", \\\"department\\\", \\\"salary\\\"], referenceSet: [] }],\\n          }));\\n        }\\n      });\\n      this.ws.on(\\\"message\\\", (data) => this.onFrame(data, t, resolve));\\n      this.ws.once(\\\"error\\\", (e) => { clearTimeout(t); reject(e); });\\n      this.ws.on(\\\"close\\\", () => {\\n        if (!stopped && this.state !== \\\"closed-intentional\\\") {\\n          this.state = \\\"closed-unexpected\\\";\\n          metrics.subscriptionsClosedByServer += 1;\\n          log({ event: \\\"unexpected_close\\\", subscription: this.index });\\n        }\\n      });\\n    });\\n    this.state = \\\"open\\\";\\n    metrics.perSubscription[this.index] = { port: this.port, id: this.id };\\n  }\\n  onFrame(data, ackTimer, ackResolve) {\\n    let frame;\\n    try { frame = JSON.parse(data.toString()); } catch { return; }\\n    if (frame.type === \\\"subscribeResponses\\\") {\\n      const r = frame.responses?.[0];\\n      if (r?.type === \\\"success\\\") {\\n        this.id = r.id;\\n        this.cursor = r.cursor;\\n        clearTimeout(ackTimer);\\n        metrics.subscriptionsOpened += 1;\\n        ackResolve();\\n      } else {\\n        clearTimeout(ackTimer);\\n        metrics.subscriptionsFailedToOpen += 1;\\n        metrics.errorFrames.push({ subscription: this.index, frame });\\n        ackResolve();\\n      }\\n      return;\\n    }\\n    if (frame.type === \\\"objectSetChanged\\\") {\\n      this.frames += 1;\\n      metrics.deliveredFrames += 1;\\n      const ids = frame.eventIds ?? [];\\n      for (const eid of ids) {\\n        if (seenEventIds.has(eid)) metrics.duplicateEvents += 1;\\n        else seenEventIds.add(eid);\\n      }\\n      for (const u of frame.updates ?? []) {\\n        this.events += 1;\\n        metrics.deliveredEvents += 1;\\n        const pk = u.object?.employeeId ?? u.primaryKey;\\n        if (pk && pendingSourceEvents.has(pk)) {\\n          metrics.deliveryLatencyMs.push(performance.now() - pendingSourceEvents.get(pk));\\n          pendingSourceEvents.delete(pk);\\n        }\\n      }\\n      if (frame.cursor) {\\n        this.cursor = frame.cursor;\\n      }\\n      return;\\n    }\\n    if (frame.type === \\\"refreshObjectSet\\\") { metrics.refreshOrResyncFrames += 1; return; }\\n    if (frame.type === \\\"error\\\" || frame.type === \\\"subscriptionClosed\\\") {\\n      metrics.errorFrames.push({ subscription: this.index, frame });\\n      return;\\n    }\\n  }\\n  async reconnectWithResume() {\\n    metrics.reconnects += 1;\\n    try { this.ws?.close(); } catch {}\\n    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 250));\\n    await this.connect(true);\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nasync function main() {\\n  console.log(JSON.stringify({ sloDefinedBeforeRun: slo, configuration: { subscriptionCount, durationMs, eventIntervalMs, ports, ontology } }));\\n  // Phase A: open all subscriptions\\n  const subs = [];\\n  for (let i = 0; i < subscriptionCount; i++) {\\n    const s = new SoakSubscription(i);\\n    try { await s.connect(false); } catch (e) { metrics.subscriptionsFailedToOpen += 1; metrics.errorFrames.push({ subscription: i, open: String(e) }); }\\n    subs.push(s);\\n  }\\n  log({ event: \\\"all_subscriptions_open\\\", opened: metrics.subscriptionsOpened, failed: metrics.subscriptionsFailedToOpen });\\n\\n  let actionSeq = 0;\\n  let lastProbe = Date.now();\\n  // Phase B: steady state — generate source events; periodically reconnect a subscription\\n  while (Date.now() < deadline && !stopped) {\\n    const id = `E-SOAK-${Date.now()}-${actionSeq++}`;\\n    const port = ports[actionSeq % ports.length];\\n    const res = await apiPost(port, \\\"actions/createEmployee/apply\\\", {\\n      parameters: { employeeId: id, name: \\\"Soak Event\\\", department: \\\"Streaming\\\" },\\n      options: { mode: \\\"VALIDATE_AND_EXECUTE\\\", returnEdits: \\\"ALL\\\" },\\n    });\\n    if (res.status === 200) { metrics.sourceActions += 1; pendingSourceEvents.set(id, performance.now()); }\\n    else { metrics.sourceActionFailures += 1; metrics.errorFrames.push({ sourceAction: { status: res.status, body: res.body } }); }\\n\\n    if (Date.now() - lastProbe >= reconnectProbeEveryMs) {\\n      lastProbe = Date.now();\\n      const victim = subs[Math.floor(Math.random() * subs.length)];\\n      if (victim?.state === \\\"open\\\") {\\n        await victim.reconnectWithResume().catch((e) => metrics.errorFrames.push({ resume: String(e) }));\\n      }\\n    }\\n    // resource sample every ~30s\\n    if (actionSeq % Math.max(1, Math.round(30_000 / eventIntervalMs)) === 0) {\\n      const rss = process.memoryUsage().rss;\\n      metrics.resourceSamples.push({ at: new Date().toISOString(), harnessRssBytes: rss, pendingSourceEvents: pendingSourceEvents.size });\\n    }\\n    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, eventIntervalMs));\\n  }\\n\\n  // Phase C: quiesce — wait for in-flight events to drain\\n  const drainDeadline = Date.now() + 20_000;\\n  while (pendingSourceEvents.size > 0 && Date.now() < drainDeadline) {\\n    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 500));\\n  }\\n  metrics.lostEvents = pendingSourceEvents.size;\\n  metrics.undeliveredEventIds = [...pendingSourceEvents.keys()];\\n\\n  stopped = true;\\n  for (const s of subs) { s.state = \\\"closed-intentional\\\"; try { s.ws?.close(); } catch {} }\\n  metrics.endedAt = new Date().toISOString();\\n\\n  const lat = metrics.deliveryLatencyMs.slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);\\n  const pct = (p) => (lat.length ? lat[Math.min(lat.length - 1, Math.floor((p / 100) * lat.length))] : null);\\n  const open = subs.filter((s) => s.state === \\\"open\\\" || s.state === \\\"closed-intentional\\\").length;\\n  const result = {\\n    ...metrics,\\n    deliveryLatencyMs: { count: lat.length, p50: pct(50), p95: pct(95), p99: pct(99), max: lat[lat.length - 1] ?? null },\\n    subscriptionsHealthyAtEnd: open,\\n    verdict: {\\n      lostEventsOk: metrics.lostEvents <= 0,\\n      unauthorizedEventsOk: metrics.unauthorizedEvents === 0,\\n      cursorRegressionsOk: metrics.cursorRegressions === 0,\\n      allSubscriptionsOpened: metrics.subscriptionsOpened >= subscriptionCount && metrics.subscriptionsFailedToOpen === 0,\\n      deliveryP99Ok: (pct(99) ?? Infinity) <= slo.eventDeliveryP99Ms,\\n      noUnexpectedCloses: metrics.subscriptionsClosedByServer === 0,\\n    },\\n  };\\n  result.verdict.PASS = Object.values(result.verdict).every((v) => v === true);\\n  if (outputPath) await writeFile(outputPath, JSON.stringify({ sloDefinedBeforeRun: slo, result }, null, 2));\\n  console.log(JSON.stringify(result.verdict));\\n  process.exit(result.verdict.PASS ? 0 : 1);\\n}\\n\\nmain().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(2); });\\nEOF\\necho written\"}","call_id":"exec_command:138","internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough":{"turn_id":"019faace-8a7b-7262-be68-e99a6914e526"}}}

{"timestamp":"2026-07-28T22:39:20.763Z","type":"response_item","payload":{"type":"function_call","id":"exec_command:162","name":"exec_command","arguments":"{\"cmd\": \"cd /Users/olivierhabimana/Desktop/projects/tellus-oss-v2-verify-6454c2a && python3 - <<'EOF'\\np = 'scripts/oss-v2-subscription-soak.mjs'\\ns = open(p).read()\\n\\n# Send a proper unsubscribe frame before closing at the end of the soak,\\n# and after each reconnect probe, so server-side subscription rows close.\\ns = s.replace(\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"  async reconnectWithResume() {\\n    metrics.reconnects += 1;\\n    this.expectingReconnectClose = true;\\n    try { this.ws?.close(); } catch {}\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"  async reconnectWithResume() {\\n    metrics.reconnects += 1;\\n    this.expectingReconnectClose = true;\\n    // Resume re-binds the same durable subscription id, so no row cleanup\\n    // is needed here — just close the transport.\\n    try { this.ws?.close(); } catch {}\\\"\\\"\\\")\\n\\ns = s.replace(\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"  stopped = true;\\n  for (const s of subs) { s.state = \\\"closed-intentional\\\"; try { s.ws?.close(); } catch {} }\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"  stopped = true;\\n  // Phase D: graceful unsubscribe so the server closes durable rows.\\n  for (const s of subs) {\\n    s.state = \\\"closed-intentional\\\";\\n    try {\\n      if (s.ws && s.ws.readyState === 1 && s.id) {\\n        s.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: \\\"unsubscribe\\\", id: `soak-unsub-${s.index}`, subscriptionId: s.id }));\\n      }\\n    } catch {}\\n  }\\n  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 2_000));\\n  for (const s of subs) { try { s.ws?.close(); } catch {} }\\\"\\\"\\\")\\n\\nopen(p, 'w').write(s)\\nprint('patched')\\nEOF\\nnode --check scripts/oss-v2-subscription-soak.mjs && echo syntax-ok\\ndocker exec tellus-oss-v2-next-pg psql -U tellus -d tellus_db -c \\\"update object_set_subscription set status='closed', updated_at=now() where status='active';\\\"\"}","call_id":"exec_command:162","internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough":{"turn_id":"019faada-c7f5-7d32-b38d-fade16cffa7a"}}}

Reconnect WS with fresh auth on unexpected close (instead of only counting)\\ns = s.replace(\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"        if (!stopped && this.state !== \\\"closed-intentional\\\") {\\n          this.state = \\\"closed-unexpected\\\";\\n          metrics.subscriptionsClosedByServer += 1;\\n          log({ event: \\\"unexpected_close\\\", subscription: this.index });\\n        }\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"        if (!stopped && this.state !== \\\"closed-intentional\\\") {\\n          this.state = \\\"closed-unexpected\\\";\\n          metrics.subscriptionsClosedByServer += 1;\\n          log({ event: \\\"unexpected_close\\\", subscription: this.index });\\n          // Re-authenticate and resume from the durable cursor so the\\n          // subscription survives credential rotation mid-soak.\\n          setTimeout(() => {\\n            if (stopped) return;\\n            (async () => {\\n              try { await refreshToken(); } catch {}\\n              this.expectingReconnectClose = false;\\n              await this.connect(true).catch(() => {});\\n            })();\\n          }, 1_000);\\n        }\\\"\\\"\\\")\\n\\n# 3. Periodic proactive token refresh + source-action retry on 401\\ns = s.replace(\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"  let actionSeq = 0;\\n  let lastProbe = Date.now();\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"  let actionSeq = 0;\\n  let lastProbe = Date.now();\\n  let lastTokenRefresh = Date.now();\\\"\\\"\\\")\\n\\ns = s.replace(\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"    const res = await apiPost(port, \\\"actions/createEmployee/apply\\\", {\\n      parameters: { employeeId: id, name: \\\"Soak Event\\\", department: \\\"Streaming\\\" },\\n      options: { mode: \\\"VALIDATE_AND_EXECUTE\\\", returnEdits: \\\"ALL\\\" },\\n    });\\n    if (res.status === 200) { metrics.sourceActions += 1; pendingSourceEvents.set(id, performance.now()); }\\n    else { metrics.sourceActionFailures += 1; metrics.errorFrames.push({ sourceAction: { status: res.status, body: res.body } }); }\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"    if (tokenUrl && Date.now() - lastTokenRefresh >= tokenRefreshMs) {\\n      lastTokenRefresh = Date.now();\\n      try { await refreshToken(); } catch (e) { log({ event: \\\"token_refresh_failed\\\", error: String(e) }); }\\n    }\\n    let res = await apiPost(port, \\\"actions/createEmployee/apply\\\", {\\n      parameters: { employeeId: id, name: \\\"Soak Event\\\", department: \\\"Streaming\\\" },\\n      options: { mode: \\\"VALIDATE_AND_EXECUTE\\\", returnEdits: \\\"ALL\\\" },\\n    });\\n    if (res.status === 401 && tokenUrl) {\\n      try { await refreshToken(); } catch {}\\n      res = await apiPost(port, \\\"actions/createEmployee/apply\\\", {\\n        parameters: { employeeId: id, name: \\\"Soak Event\\\", department: \\\"Streaming\\\" },\\n        options: { mode: \\\"VALIDATE_AND_EXECUTE\\\", returnEdits: \\\"ALL\\\" },\\n      });\\n    }\\n    if (res.status === 200) { metrics.sourceActions += 1; pendingSourceEvents.set(id, performance.now()); }\\n    else { metrics.sourceActionFailures += 1; metrics.errorFrames.push({ sourceAction: { status: res.status, body: res.body } }); }\\\"\\\"\\\")\\n\\n# 4. Initial token acquisition when only URL is provided\\ns = s.replace(\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"async function main() {\\n  console.log(JSON.stringify({ sloDefinedBeforeRun: slo, configuration: { subscriptionCount, durationMs, eventIntervalMs, ports, ontology } }));\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"async function main() {\\n  if (!token) await refreshToken();\\n  console.log(JSON.stringify({ sloDefinedBeforeRun: slo, configuration: { subscriptionCount, durationMs, eventIntervalMs, ports, ontology } }));\\\"\\\"\\\")\\n\\nopen(p, 'w').write(s)\\nprint('patched')\\nEOF\\nnode --check scripts/oss-v2-subscription-soak.mjs && echo syntax-ok\"}","call_id":"exec_command:180","internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough":{"turn_id":"019fab24-2dea-7e22-9ada-6926d2b58bfb"}}}

{"timestamp":"2026-07-29T01:07:17.299Z","type":"response_item","payload":{"type":"function_call_output","call_id":"exec_command:207","output":"Chunk ID: a5d552\nWall time: 0.0434 seconds\nProcess exited with code 0\nOriginal token count: 603\nOutput:\n    clients.set(ws, state);\n\n    ws.on('pong', () => { state.isAlive = true; });\n\n    const emitDurableUpdates = async () => {\n      if (!state.security || !state.tenantId) return;\n      for (const slot of state.durableSubscriptions.values()) {\n        if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;\n        if (ws.bufferedAmount > 1_000_000) {\n          ws.close(1013, \"slow consumer\");\n          return;\n        }\n        const replay = await replayEvents(slot.subscription, slot.cursor);\n        if (replay.expired) {\n          const cursor = createSubscriptionCursor({\n            subscriptionId: slot.subscription.id,\n            tenantId: slot.subscription.tenantId,\n            userId: slot.subscription.ownerUserId,\n            sequence: replay.cursor,\n          });\n          for (const objectType of slot.subscription.dependencyTypes) {\n            ws.send(JSON.stringify({\n              type: \"refreshObjectSet\",\n              id: slot.subscription.id,\n              objectType,\n              cursor,\n            }));\n          }\n          slot.cursor = replay.cursor;\n          continue;\n        }\n        const updates = [];\n        const eventIds: string[] = [];\n        for (const event of replay.events) {\n          const object = await loadAuthorizedEventObject({\n            subscription: slot.subscription,\n            event,\n            security: state.security,\n          });\n          updates.push({\n            type: \"object\",\n            object:\n              object ?? {\n                __apiName: event.objectType,\n                __primaryKey: event.primaryKey,\n                ...(event.objectRid ? { __rid: event.objectRid } : {}),\n              },\n            state:\n              event.state === \"REMOVED\" || !object\n                ? \"REMOVED\"\n                : \"ADDED_OR_UPDATED\",\n          });\n          eventIds.push(event.eventId);\n        }\n        if (updates.length > 0) {\n          const cursor = createSubscriptionCursor({\n            subscriptionId: slot.subscription.id,\n            tenantId: slot.subscription.tenantId,\n            userId: slot.subscription.ownerUserId,\n            sequence: replay.cursor,\n          });\n          ws.send(JSON.stringify({\n            type: \"objectSetChanged\",\n            id: slot.subscription.id,\n            updates,\n            cursor,\n            eventIds,\n          }));\n          slot.cursor = replay.cursor;\n        }\n      }\n","internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough":{"turn_id":"019fab64-2373-7cc1-bae8-fd97edac3a02"}}}
